rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003eEvaluation\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e - Leverage LLMs to benchmark your application's performance using response and retrieval evals.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/datasets-and-experiments/overview-datasets\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003eDatasets\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e - Create versioned datasets of examples for experimentation, evaluation, and fine-tuning.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/datasets-and-experiments/overview-datasets#experiments\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003eExperiments\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e - Track and evaluate changes to prompts, LLMs, and retrieval.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/prompt-engineering/overview-prompts\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003ePlayground\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e- Optimize prompts, compare models, adjust parameters, and replay traced LLM calls.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/prompt-engineering/overview-prompts/prompt-management\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003ePrompt Management\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e- Manage and test prompt changes systematically using version control, tagging, and experimentation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/pxi\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003ePXI (Phoenix Intelligence)\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e - An AI engineering agent built into Phoenix for debugging traces, iterating on prompts, and navigating the product.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/remote-mcp\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cem\u003eRemote MCP Server\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/a\u003e - Connect Claude Code, Cursor, and other MCP clients directly to your Phoenix instance's \u003ccode\u003e/mcp\u003c/code\u003e endpoint to query traces, datasets, experiments, and more.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n </p>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix is an open-source AI observability platform designed for experimentation, evaluation, and troubleshooting. It provides:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/llm-traces" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Tracing</em></strong></a> - Trace your LLM application's runtime using OpenTelemetry-based instrumentation.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/evaluation/llm-evals" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Evaluation</em></strong></a> - Leverage LLMs to benchmark your application's performance using response and retrieval evals.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/datasets-and-experiments/overview-datasets" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Datasets</em></strong></a> - Create versioned datasets of examples for experimentation, evaluation, and fine-tuning.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/datasets-and-experiments/overview-datasets#experiments" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Experiments</em></strong></a> - Track and evaluate changes to prompts, LLMs, and retrieval.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/prompt-engineering/overview-prompts" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Playground</em></strong></a>- Optimize prompts, compare models, adjust parameters, and replay traced LLM calls.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/prompt-engineering/overview-prompts/prompt-management" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Prompt Management</em></strong></a>- Manage and test prompt changes systematically using version control, tagging, and experimentation.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/pxi" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>PXI (Phoenix Intelligence)</em></strong></a> - An AI engineering agent built into Phoenix for debugging traces, iterating on prompts, and navigating the product.</li>
<li><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/remote-mcp" rel="nofollow"><strong><em>Remote MCP Server</em></strong></a> - Connect Claude Code, Cursor, and other MCP clients directly to your Phoenix instance's <code>/mcp</code> endpoint to query traces, datasets, experiments, and more.</li>
</ul>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
    
</p>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix is vendor and language agnostic with out-of-the-box support for popular frameworks (<a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/openai-agents-sdk" rel="nofollow">OpenAI Agents SDK</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/python/claude-agent-sdk" rel="nofollow">Claude Agent SDK</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/langchain" rel="nofollow">LangGraph</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/vercel-ai-sdk" rel="nofollow">Vercel AI SDK</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/typescript/mastra" rel="nofollow">Mastra</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/crewai" rel="nofollow">CrewAI</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/llamaindex" rel="nofollow">LlamaIndex</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/dspy" rel="nofollow">DSPy</a>) and LLM providers (<a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/openai" rel="nofollow">OpenAI</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/anthropic" rel="nofollow">Anthropic</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/google-genai" rel="nofollow">Google GenAI</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/llm-providers/google-gen-ai/google-adk-tracing" rel="nofollow">Google ADK</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/bedrock" rel="nofollow">AWS Bedrock</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/python/openrouter" rel="nofollow">OpenRouter</a>, <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/integrations-tracing/litellm" rel="nofollow">LiteLLM</a>, and more). For details on auto-instrumentation, check out the <a href="https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference">OpenInference</a> project.</p>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix runs practically anywhere, including your local machine, a containerized deployment, or in the cloud. See <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/environments" rel="nofollow">Environments</a> for a walkthrough of each option, or jump straight into the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/get-started/get-started-tracing" rel="nofollow">Tracing Quickstart</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run Locally</h2><a id="user-content-run-locally" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run Locally" href="#run-locally"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install Phoenix via <code>pip</code> or <code>conda</code> and have a fully functional Phoenix. For all installation and hosting options, see the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix" rel="nofollow">install guide</a>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install arize-phoenix
phoenix serve"><pre>pip install arize-phoenix
phoenix serve</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or run it with no install using <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/" rel="nofollow"><code>uvx</code></a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="uvx arize-phoenix serve"><pre>uvx arize-phoenix serve</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Trace Your Application</h2><a id="user-content-trace-your-application" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Trace Your Application" href="#trace-your-application"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The fastest way to send traces is to let your <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/developer-tools/coding-agents" rel="nofollow">coding agent</a> (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and others) instrument your app. From your project directory, run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @arizeai/phoenix-cli setup
# or, with Phoenix installed: px setup"><pre>npx @arizeai/phoenix-cli setup
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or, with Phoenix installed: px setup</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Setup detects your framework and LLM provider, installs the right <a href="https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference">OpenInference</a> instrumentation, and wires up trace export. Prefer to wire it up in code? See the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/tracing/llm-traces" rel="nofollow">tracing documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deploy</h2><a id="user-content-deploy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deploy" href="#deploy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix container images are available via <a href="https://hub.docker.com/r/arizephoenix/phoenix" rel="nofollow">Docker Hub</a> and can be deployed using Docker or Kubernetes via the <a href="https://github.com/Arize-ai/phoenix/tree/main/helm">Helm chart</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For Docker Compose, Kubernetes/Helm, and other deployment options, see the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/self-hosting" rel="nofollow">self-hosting documentation</a>.</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a href="https://railway.app/template/PTHRoq?referralCode=Xe2txW" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/88cb27bf937b98276737ba7888269a1bc9495d8670f63e1acc908b3442841114/68747470733a2f2f7261696c7761792e636f6d2f627574746f6e2e737667" alt="Deploy on Railway" height="30" data-canonical-src="https://railway.com/button.svg" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 30px;"></a>

</p>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-note" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-info mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 8a8 8 0 1 1 16 0A8 8 0 0 1 0 8Zm8-6.5a6.5 6.5 0 1 0 0 13 6.5 6.5 0 0 0 0-13ZM6.5 7.75A.75.75 0 0 1 7.25 7h1a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.75h.25a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h.25v-2h-.25a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75ZM8 6a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Note</p><p dir="auto">The Google Cloud button builds Phoenix from source in Cloud Shell rather than deploying the prebuilt Docker Hub image. The Azure template serves plain HTTP (Azure Container Instances does not terminate TLS) — front it with a TLS proxy such as an Application Gateway before production use.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Packages</h2><a id="user-content-packages" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Packages" href="#packages"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>arize-phoenix</code> package includes the entire Phoenix platform. However, if you have deployed the Phoenix platform, there are lightweight Python sub-packages and TypeScript packages that can be used in conjunction with the platform.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tracing Integrations</h2><a id="user-content-tracing-integrations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tracing Integrations" href="#tracing-integrations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix is built on top of OpenTelemetry and is vendor, language, and framework agnostic. For details about tracing integrations and example applications, see the <a href="https://github.com/Arize-ai/openinference">OpenInference</a> project and the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations" rel="nofollow">integrations documentation</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Span Processors</h2><a id="user-content-span-processors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Span Processors" href="#span-processors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Sandboxes</h2><a id="user-content-sandboxes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Sandboxes" href="#sandboxes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run Phoenix <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/evaluation/server-evals/code-evaluators" rel="nofollow">code evaluators</a> in hosted sandbox providers for kernel-level isolation and runtime dependency installation.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">For Humans and Coding Agents</h2><a id="user-content-for-humans-and-coding-agents" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: For Humans and Coding Agents" href="#for-humans-and-coding-agents"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Phoenix is built to be driven by people and by AI coding agents alike. Three surfaces let agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and others) work with your traces, datasets, and experiments:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/sdk-api-reference/typescript/arizeai-phoenix-cli" rel="nofollow">CLI</a></strong> — <code>npx @arizeai/phoenix-cli</code> fetches traces, datasets, and experiments and instruments your app (<code>setup</code>), so an agent can pull context and act on it from the terminal.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code/skills" rel="nofollow">Skills</a></strong> — <a href="/Arize-ai/phoenix/blob/main/.agents/skills"><code>.agents/skills/</code></a> packages workflows that teach agents how to debug, evaluate, and trace with Phoenix.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/remote-mcp" rel="nofollow">Remote MCP Server</a></strong> — connect any MCP client to your instance's <code>/mcp</code> endpoint to query Phoenix directly.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/integrations/developer-tools/coding-agents" rel="nofollow">coding agents documentation</a> for setup and usage.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Skill</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><a href="/Arize-ai/phoenix/blob/main/.agents/skills/phoenix-cli">phoenix-cli</a></td>
<td>Debug LLM applications using the Phoenix CLI — fetch traces, analyze errors, review experiments, and query the GraphQL API</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/Arize-ai/phoenix/blob/main/.agents/skills/phoenix-evals">phoenix-evals</a></td>
<td>Build and run evaluators for AI/LLM applications using Phoenix</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><a href="/Arize-ai/phoenix/blob/main/.agents/skills/phoenix-tracing">phoenix-tracing</a></td>
<td>OpenInference semantic conventions and instrumentation for tracing LLM applications</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security &amp; Privacy</h2><a id="user-content-security--privacy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security &amp; Privacy" href="#security--privacy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We take data security and privacy very seriously. For more details, see our <a href="https://arize.com/docs/phoenix/self-hosting/security/privacy" rel="nofollow">Security and Privacy documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Telemetry</h3><a id="user-content-telemetry" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Telemetry" href="#telemetry"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, Phoenix collects basic web analytics (e.g., page views, UI interactions) to help us understand how Phoenix is used and improve the product. <strong>None of your trace data, evaluation results, or any sensitive information is ever collected.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">You can opt-out of telemetry by setting the environment variable: <code>PHOENIX_TELEMETRY_ENABLED=false</code></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Community</h2><a id="user-content-community" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Community" href="#community"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
